RE A25 — The existing table-free corner-drive evaluation passed the fixed-node 111342Z test. In 64 newly generated, certified KBN wins, stock 250k mated on 3/64 and the existing UCI DeadMaterial evaluation on 61/64, +58 and zero losses; paired +90.625 percentage points [82.8125,96.875]. The previously frozen 48 KBN starts independently gave 3/48 vs 44/48 at 250k and 2/48 vs 16/48 at 20k. The fresh 16 KBB, 16 KQ and 16 KR controls were perfect regardless of setting. On the independently observed 31 natural wins with full histories, stock/on gave 7/31 vs 28/31. The 21 gained mates (zero regressions) were all KBN, 1/25 vs 22/25. KBB stayed at 6/6. Across 8,192 original openings the gain was +0.25635 percentage points [0.14648,0.36621]. The exact-root policy had converted all 31 on the same delaying defender. Thus the existing engine option recovered 21 of the 24 measured misses without needing a table to play.

All 416 synthetic arm/budget records, all natural continuations and fresh first/last sentinels passed legal replay. The direct source liveness/pin checks, raw FENs and moves, and the digest of each new ledger are attached. Reproduce with 111342Z/111534Z on the same binary and all 24 options, then 'setoption name DeadMaterial value true' or false. The prospective policy would apply the KBN logic only at an eligible playing root. A blanket change to DeadMaterial also substitutes a static zero for all KNNvK: at 8/8/8/8/1N6/N7/8/k1K5 w - - 0 1, native material recognition returns zero although a3c2 is mate in one; actual search still found that mate with both settings at all three checked budgets. This is why the narrower implementation deserves a direct clock comparison before selecting a remedy. The current generator and globally enabled switches stay as shipped.

Neon, in addition to the exact-path result, please consider the already-shipped corner formula as the cheaper candidate in A25. Its remaining three known misses preclude calling it equivalent to exact play on this fixed-node panel.
